INSURANCE COMPANIES AUTHORIZED TO DO BUSINESS IN LOUISIANA 

Corrected Through June 15, 1953

STATE OF LOUISIANA CONFIDENCE Compiled by 

WADE O. MARTIN, JR. 

Secretary of State and 

Insurance Commissioner

The Secretary of State of the State of Louisiana has been duly appointed agent in the State of Louisiana, by each foreign or alien insurer qualified in this State, as the true and lawful attorney in the State of Louisiana upon whom all lawful process in any action or proceedings against such insurer may be served, which service shall constitute service on such insurer. (Title 22 Louisiana 

Revised Statutes of 1950.)

All fraternal societies authorized to do business in the 

State of Louisiana have designated the Secretary of

State to be their true and lawful attorney upon whom all lawful process in any action or proceeding against them shall be served. (Title 22 Louisiana Revised Statutes of 1950.)
